os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'dataset.csv')\n    19\t    opt_vals = {}\n    20\t    if os.path.exists(dataset_path):\n    21\t        with open(dataset_path, 'r') as f:\n    22\t            reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    23\t            for row in reader:\n    24\t                key = (int(row['function']), int(row['instance']), int(row['dimension']))\n    25\t                opt_vals[key] = float(row['opt_val'])\n    26\t    return opt_vals\n    27\t\n    28\t\n    29\tdef compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val):\n    30\t    \"\"\"\u8ba1\u7b97 score = (initial_best - best) / (initial_best - opt)\u3002\n    31\t    \n    32\t    \u5176\u4e2d\uff1a\n    33\t    - initial_best = initial_gbest_f\uff08PSO\u521d\u59cb\u5316\u540e\u79cd\u7fa4\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    34\t    - best = gbest_f\uff08PSO\u8fd0\u884c\u7ed3\u675f\u540e\u7684\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    35\t    - opt = opt_val\uff08\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\uff09\n    36\t    \n    37\t    \u8fd4\u56de\u622a\u65ad\u5230 [0, 1] \u7684 score\u3002\n    38\t    \"\"\"    \n    39\t    if abs(initial_gbest_f - opt_val) < 1e-12:\n    40\t        return 1.0 if gbest_f <= opt_val + 1e-12 else 0.0\n    41\t    \n    42\t    score = (initial_gbest_f - gbest_f) / (initial_gbest_f - opt_val)\n    43\t    return max(0.0, min(1.0, score))\n    44\t\n    45\t\n    46\tdef run_de_and_score(inputs, log_fn=print, mode='test', controller=None, seed=None):\n    47\t    \"\"\"\u8fd0\u884c DQN \u63a7\u5236\u7684 DE \u5e76\u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u3002\n    48\t    \n    49\t    Args:\n    50\t        inputs: dict from domains.bbob_unconstrained.utils.format_input_dict\n    51\t        Expected keys: 'problem_id','dimension','instance'\n    52\t        log_fn: logging function\n    53\t        mode: 'train' for training DQN (updates model), 'test' for evaluation only\n    54\t        controller: optional external controller (shared across problems)\n    55\t        seed: random seed for DE initialization\n    56\t    \n    57\t    Returns:\n    58\t        prediction (string) in format \"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\"\n    59\t    \"\"\"\n    60\t    # lazy import cocoex and DE module from metabbo\n    61\t    import cocoex\n    62\t\n    63\t    this_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..', '..', 'metabbo')\n    64\t    # \u5c06 metabbo \u76ee\u5f55\u6dfb\u52a0\u5230 sys.path\uff0c\u4ee5\u4fbf\u6a21\u5757\u5185\u7684\u76f8\u5bf9\u5bfc\u5165\u53ef\u4ee5\u6b63\u5e38\u5de5\u4f5c\n    65\t    if this_dir not in __import__('sys').path:\n    66\t        __import__('sys').path.insert(0, this_dir)\n    67\t    # load local ec_algorithm module\n    68\t    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ec_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'ec_algorithm.py'))\n    69\t    ec_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n    70\t    spec.loader.exec_module(ec_mod)\n    71\t    \n    72\t    # Create or use provided controller\n    73\t    if controller is None:\n    74\t        # load meta learning implementations\n    75\t        spec2 = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('meta_mod', os.path.join(this_dir, 'meta_learning.py'))\n    76\t        meta_mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec2)\n    77\t        spec2.loader.exec_module(meta_mod)\n    78\t        model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n    79\t        controller = meta_mod.DiscreteDQNController(model_path=model_path, training=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    80\t    \n    81\t    # prepare suite filter (dimension + instance)\n    82\t    dim = int(inputs.get('dimension', 10))\n    83\t    inst = int(inputs.get('instance', 1))\n    84\t    max_evals = max(1000, dim * 500)\n    85\t\n    86\t    # \u4ece problem_id \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u4e2d\u63d0\u53d6 function \u7f16\u53f7\uff08\u4f8b\u5982 f1_i1 -> 1\uff09\n    87\t    problem_id_str = inputs.get('problem_id', 'f1_i1')\n    88\t    problem_id = int(str(problem_id_str).replace('f', '').split('_')[0])\n    89\t\n    90\t    suite = cocoex.Suite('bbob', '', f'dimensions: {dim} instance_indices:{inst} function_indices: {problem_id}')\n    91\t    # pick the matching problem from the suite\n    92\t    problem = next(iter(suite))\n    93\t\n    94\t    # run DE algorithm using DEOptimizer to get stats\n    95\t    try:\n    96\t        de_opt = ec_mod.DEOptimizer(problem.dimension, getattr(problem, 'lower_bounds', None), getattr(problem, 'upper_bounds', None), max_evals, params={'F': 0.5, 'CR': 0.5}, controller=controller, recorder=None, swarm_size=40)\n    97\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4f1a\u66f4\u65b0 DQN \u6a21\u578b\uff0c\u6d4b\u8bd5\u6a21\u5f0f\u53ea\u4f7f\u7528\u6a21\u578b\n    98\t        gbest, stats = de_opt.optimize(problem, train=(mode == 'train'), seed=seed)\n    99\t        gbest_f = stats.get('gbest_f', None)\n   100\t        initial_gbest_f = stats.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f)\n   101\t        \n   102\t        # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u4fdd\u5b58\u6a21\u578b\uff08\u53ea\u5728 controller \u662f\u5185\u90e8\u521b\u5efa\u65f6\u4fdd\u5b58\uff09\n   103\t        if mode == 'train' and controller is not None and hasattr(controller, 'net'):\n   104\t            import torch\n   105\t            model_path = os.path.join(this_dir, 'dqn_model.pt')\n   106\t            torch.save(controller.net.state_dict(), model_path)\n   107\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Model saved to {model_path}\")\n   108\t    except Exception as e:\n   109\t        return f'error: DE run failed: {e}'\n   110\t\n   111\t    # \u8ba1\u7b97 RI \u5206\u6570\n   112\t    # \u52a0\u8f7d\u7406\u8bba\u6700\u4f18\u503c\n   113\t    opt_vals = load_opt_vals()\n   114\t    opt_val_key = (problem_id, inst, dim)\n   115\t    opt_val = opt_vals.get(opt_val_key, None)\n   116\t    \n   117\t    # \u4f7f\u7528 RI \u8bc4\u5206\u65b9\u5f0f\n   118\t    score = compute_ri_score(initial_gbest_f, gbest_f, opt_val)\n   119\t    \n   120\t    mode_str = \"TRAIN\" if mode == 'train' else \"TEST\"\n   121\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} Score: {score}\")\n   122\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} gbest_f: {gbest_f}\")\n   123\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} initial_gbest_f: {initial_gbest_f}\")\n   124\t    log_fn(f\"[{mode_str}] f{problem_id} opt_val: {opt_val}\")\n   125\t    \n   126\t    # \u8bad\u7ec3\u6a21\u5f0f\u4e0b\u6536\u96c6 loss \u548c return\n   127\t    if mode == 'train':\n   128\t        training_loss = controller.get_total_loss() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_loss') else None\n   129\t        sweep_return = controller.get_total_reward() if hasattr(controller, 'get_total_reward') else None\n   130\t        if training_loss is not None:\n   131\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN loss: {training_loss:.6f}\")\n   132\t        if sweep_return is not None:\n   133\t            log_fn(f\"[TRAIN] Total DQN return: {sweep_return:.4f}\")\n   134\t        return {\n   135\t            'score': score,\n   136\t            'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   137\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   138\t            'opt_val': opt_val,\n   139\t            'training_loss': training_loss,\n   140\t            'sweep_return': sweep_return,\n   141\t            'controller': controller\n   142\t        }\n   143\t    \n   144\t    # \u8fd4\u56de\u683c\u5f0f\uff1a\"score|gbest_f|initial_gbest_f|opt_val\" \u4ee5\u4fbf report.py \u89e3\u6790\n   145\t    prediction = f\"{score}|{gbest_f}|{initial_gbest_f}|{opt_val}\"\n   146\t\n   147\t    # \u4e5f\u8fd4\u56de dict \u683c\u5f0f\u4f9b\u6d4b\u8bd5\u811a\u672c\u4f7f\u7528\n   148\t    return {\n   149\t        'score': score,\n   150\t        'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n   151\t        'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   152\t        'opt_val': opt_val,\n   153\t        'training_loss': None,\n   154\t        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass StaticController(ParamController):\n    36\t    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\n    37\t        self.params = dict(params)\n    38\t\n    39\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    40\t        return None\n    41\t\n    42\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    43\t        return dict(self.params)\n    44\t\n    45\t\n    46\tclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\n    47\t    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\n    48\t    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\n    49\t    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\n    50\t        self.w_start = w_start\n    51\t        self.w_end = w_end\n    52\t        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\n    53\t\n    54\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    55\t        self.it = 0\n    56\t\n    57\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    58\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n    59\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\n    60\t        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\n    61\t        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\n    62\t        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\n    63\t        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\n    64\t        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\n    65\t        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\n    66\t        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\n    67\t        return {'w': w, 'c1': c1, 'c2': c2, 'F': F, 'CR': CR}\n    68\t\n    69\t\n    70\tclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\n    71\t    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\n    72\t\n    73\t    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\n    74\t      use 'rand/1' strategy to explore broadly.\n    75\t    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\n    76\t      good building blocks), switch to 'jade' for fast convergence.\n    77\t    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\n    78\t    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\n    79\t      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\n    80\t      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\n    81\t    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\n    82\t      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\n    83\t    \"\"\"\n    84\t    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\n    85\t                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\n    86\t                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\n    87\t        self.f_start = f_start\n    88\t        self.f_end = f_end\n    89\t        self.cr_start = cr_start\n    90\t        self.cr_end = cr_end\n    91\t        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\n    92\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    93\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    94\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    95\t\n    96\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    97\t        self.it = 0\n    98\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    99\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   100\t        self.stagnation_count = 0\n   101\t\n   102\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   103\t        self.it = observation.get('iteration', self.it + 1)\n   104\t        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get('max_evals', 10000) / 100))\n   105\t        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\n   106\t\n   107\t        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\n   112\t        current_gbest = observation.get('gbest_f', None)\n   113\t        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\n   114\t            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\n   115\t                self.stagnation_count = 0\n   116\t            else:\n   117\t                self.stagnation_count += 1\n   118\t        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\n   119\t\n   120\t        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\n   121\t        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\n   122\t            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\n   123\t            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\n   124\t            self.stagnation_count = 0\n   125\t\n   126\t        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\n   127\t        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\n   128\t            strategy = 'rand/1'\n   129\t        else:\n   130\t            strategy = 'jade'\n   131\t\n   132\t        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\n   133\t        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\n   134\t        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\n   135\t        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\n   136\t        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\n   137\t        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\n   138\t\n   139\t        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\n   140\t        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\n   141\t        current_gbest_cv = observation.get('gbest_cv', None)\n   142\t        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\n   143\t            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\n   144\t\n   145\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR), 'strategy': strategy,\n   146\t                'epsilon': float(epsilon)}\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`, `undo_edit`.'}, 'file_text': {'description': 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.', 'type': 'string'}, 'insert_line': {'description': 'Required parameter of `insert` command. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\n\n<json>\n    {\n        \"tool_name\": \"bash\",\n        \"tool_input\": {'command': \"sed -n '1,220p' /hada/metabbo/reference_jade.py\"},\n        \"tool_output\": \"\"\"\"Reference JADE implementation compatible with the DEOptimizer interface.\n\nThis file provides a COMPLETE, syntactically valid JADE optimizer that can be\ncopied into ec_algorithm.py. It is provided as a reference template because\npartial edits to the existing DE loop often result in broken syntax.\n\nJADE: Adaptive Differential Evolution with Optional External Archive\nReference: Zhang & Sanderson (2009)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n_config_seed = None\nsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\nfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n\n\ndef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n\n    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n        if x.ndim == 1:\n            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n        result = problem.func(x)\n        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    else:\n        return float(problem(x))\n\n\nclass DEOptimizer:\n    \"\"\"JADE optimizer with the same interface as the original DEOptimizer.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, dim, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals,\n                 params=None, controller=None, recorder=None, swarm_size=None):\n        self.dim = int(dim)\n        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n        self.controller = controller\n        self.recorder = recorder\n        self.stats = {}\n        # Default pop size 40 to match evaluator, or use swarm_size\n        self.pop_size = int(swarm_size) if swarm_size is not None else 40\n        # F and CR defaults\n        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.7))\n        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n\n        # Bounds\n        lb = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n        ub = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n        if lb.ndim == 0:\n            lb = np.full(self.dim, float(lb))\n        if ub.ndim == 0:\n            ub = np.full(self.dim, float(ub))\n        self.lb = lb\n        self.ub = ub\n\n    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None):\n        \"\"\"Run JADE until max_evals is reached.\n\n        Returns:\n            (gbest_list, stats_dict)\n        \"\"\"\n        dim = self.dim\n        lb = self.lb\n        ub = self.ub\n        pop_size = self.pop_size\n\n        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n\n        # ---- Initialization ----\n        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, dim))\n        if x0 is not None:\n            x0_arr = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).flatten()\n            if x0_arr.shape[0] == dim:\n                pop[0] = np.clip(x0_arr, lb, ub)\n\n        evals = 0\n        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n        for i in range(pop_size):\n            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                break\n            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n            evals += 1\n\n        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n        prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n        # ---- JADE memory and archive ----\n        archive = np.empty((0, dim), dtype=float)\n        archive_capacity = max(pop_size * 2, 20)\n        mu_F = float(self.F)\n        mu_CR = float(self.CR)\n        c = 0.1\n\n        iteration = 0\n        while evals < self.max_evals:\n            iteration += 1\n\n            # Controller-provided F/CR are blended into the adaptation memory\n            params = dict(self.params)\n            if self.controller is not None:\n                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n                    params.update(step_params)\n            base_F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n            base_CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n            base_F = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_F + 0.5 * mu_F))\n            base_CR = min(1.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * base_CR + 0.5 * mu_CR))\n\n            # p-best selection pool (shrinks as budget is consumed)\n            progress = evals / max(1, self.max_evals)\n            p = max(0.05, 0.2 * (1.0 - progress))\n            n_pbest = max(2, int(pop_size * p))\n            sorted_idx = np.argsort(fitness)\n\n            # Archive + population pool for x_r2\n            archive_pop = np.vstack([pop, archive]) if len(archive) > 0 else pop\n            n_arch = len(archive_pop)\n\n            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n            S_F = []\n            S_CR = []\n\n            for i in range(pop_size):\n                # F_i drawn from Cauchy around mu_F, truncated\n                F_i = base_F + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\n                F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\n                # CR_i drawn from Normal around mu_CR, truncated\n                CR_i = base_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn()\n                CR_i = float(np.clip(CR_i, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n                # Current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n                pbest_idx = sorted_idx[rng.randint(0, n_pbest)]\n                r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n                while r1 == i:\n                    r1 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n                if n_arch > 1:\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, n_arch)\n                    x_r2 = archive_pop[r2]\n                else:\n                    r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n                    while r2 == i or r2 == r1:\n                        r2 = rng.randint(0, pop_size)\n                    x_r2 = pop[r2]\n\n                mutant = pop[i] + F_i * (pop[pbest_idx] - pop[i]) + F_i * (pop[r1] - x_r2)\n\n                # Boundary repair: reflect\n                for _ in range(5):\n                    below = mutant < lb\n                    above = mutant > ub\n                    if not np.any(below) and not np.any(above):\n                        break\n                    mutant = np.where(below, 2 * lb - mutant, mutant)\n                    mutant = np.where(above, 2 * ub - mutant, mutant)\n                mutant = np.clip(mutant, lb, ub)\n\n                # Binomial crossover\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, dim)\n                mask = rng.random(dim) < CR_i\n                mask[j_rand] = True\n                trial = np.where(mask, mutant, pop[i])\n\n                # Evaluation and selection\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n                    break\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, trial)\n                evals += 1\n\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n                    # Save the replaced individual to the external archive\n                    if len(archive) < archive_capacity:\n                        archive = np.vstack([archive, pop[i].reshape(1, -1)])\n                    else:\n                        ridx = rng.randint(0, archive_capacity)\n                        archive[ridx] = pop[i]\n                    new_pop[i] = trial\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n                    S_F.append(F_i)\n                    S_CR.append(CR_i)\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\n                        gbest = trial.copy()\n\n            pop = new_pop\n\n            # Update success-history memory (Lehmer mean for F, arithmetic for CR)\n            if len(S_F) > 0:\n                S_F_arr = np.array(S_F)\n                S_CR_arr = np.array(S_CR)\n                denom = np.sum(S_F_arr)\n                if denom > 0:\n                    mu_F = (1.0 - c) * mu_F + c * np.sum(S_F_arr ** 2) / denom\n                mu_CR = (1.0 - c) * mu_CR + c * np.mean(S_CR_arr)\n                mu_F = float(np.clip(mu_F, 0.1, 1.0))\n                mu_CR = float(np.clip(mu_CR, 0.0, 1.0))\n\n            # Controller reward / transition storage\n            if self.controller is not None and train:\n                improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f,\n                            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, (evals >= self.max_evals))\n            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n\n        self.stats = {\n            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\"\n    }\n    </json>",
